# TEMPLATES.md — ColorLib Recreation Checklist

> Source: [colorlib.com](https://colorlib.com/wp/templates/) — free website templates catalog.

> Each entry is a design to recreate in this monorepo (Vite + React + Tailwind + TypeScript) under a **different name**, one template per app in `apps/`.

> Original design credit: ColorLib (recreated from scratch, no assets copied).


- **Total templates:** 1646
- **Categories:** 79
- **Status legend:** `[ ]` not started · `[~]` in progress · `[x]` done
